  317 data PerpetualFuturesInterpolationType = PerpetualFuturesPiecewiseConstant
  318                                        | PerpetualFuturesLinearInterpolation
  319                                        | PerpetualFuturesCubicSpline
  320   deriving (Show,Eq,Read)
  321 instance Enum PerpetualFuturesInterpolationType where
  322   succ PerpetualFuturesPiecewiseConstant = PerpetualFuturesLinearInterpolation
  323   succ PerpetualFuturesLinearInterpolation = PerpetualFuturesCubicSpline
  324   succ PerpetualFuturesCubicSpline = error "PerpetualFuturesInterpolationType.succ: PerpetualFuturesCubicSpline has no successor"
  325 
  326   pred PerpetualFuturesLinearInterpolation = PerpetualFuturesPiecewiseConstant
  327   pred PerpetualFuturesCubicSpline = PerpetualFuturesLinearInterpolation
  328   pred PerpetualFuturesPiecewiseConstant = error "PerpetualFuturesInterpolationType.pred: PerpetualFuturesPiecewiseConstant has no predecessor"
  329 
  330   enumFromTo from to = go from
  331     where
  332       end = fromEnum to
  333       go v = case compare (fromEnum v) end of
  334                  LT -> v : go (succ v)
  335                  EQ -> [v]
  336                  GT -> []
  337 
  338   enumFrom from = enumFromTo from PerpetualFuturesCubicSpline
  339 
  340   fromEnum PerpetualFuturesPiecewiseConstant = 0
  341   fromEnum PerpetualFuturesLinearInterpolation = 1
  342   fromEnum PerpetualFuturesCubicSpline = 2
  343 
  344   toEnum 0 = PerpetualFuturesPiecewiseConstant
  345   toEnum 1 = PerpetualFuturesLinearInterpolation
  346   toEnum 2 = PerpetualFuturesCubicSpline
  347   toEnum unmatched = error ("PerpetualFuturesInterpolationType.toEnum: Cannot match " ++ show unmatched)

  461 -- |discounts a bond's cash flows off a yield term structure
  462 discountingBondEngine :: (GenYieldTermStructure y) -> (Maybe Bool) -- ^includeSettlementDateFlows
  463  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
  464 discountingBondEngine a1 a2 =
  465   withYieldTermStructure a1 $ \a1' -> 
  466   let {a2' = fromMaybeBool a2} in 
  467   preErrorCheck $ \a3' -> 
  468   discountingBondEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' >>= \res ->
  469   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
  470   errorCheck  a3'>>
  471   return (res')
  472 
  473 
  474 
  475 -- |Discounts perpetual-futures cashflows to the curves' reference date. The
  476 -- three funding vectors must be non-empty and have identical lengths. The
  477 -- engine supports only 'PerpetualFuturesLinear' and 'PerpetualFuturesInverse'
  478 -- payoffs; QuantLib rejects a Quanto payoff at pricing time.
  479 discountingPerpetualFuturesEngine
  480   :: GenYieldTermStructure y1 -> GenYieldTermStructure y2 -> GenQuote q
  481   -> NonEmpty (Double, Double, Double) -- ^@(fundingTime, fundingRate, interestRateDiff)@
  482   -> PerpetualFuturesInterpolationType -> Double -> IO PricingEngine
  483 discountingPerpetualFuturesEngine domestic foreignCurve spot funding interpolation maxT =
  484   qlDiscountingPerpetualFuturesEngine domestic foreignCurve spot times rates diffs interpolation maxT
  485   where (times, rates, diffs) = unzip3 (toList funding)
  486 
  487 qlDiscountingPerpetualFuturesEngine :: (GenYieldTermStructure y1) -- ^domesticDiscountCurve
  488  -> (GenYieldTermStructure y2) -- ^foreignDiscountCurve
  489  -> (GenQuote q) -- ^assetSpot
  490  -> ([Double]) -- ^fundingTimes
  491  -> ([Double]) -- ^fundingRates
  492  -> ([Double]) -- ^interestRateDiffs
  493  -> (PerpetualFuturesInterpolationType) -- ^fundingInterpType
  494  -> (Double) -- ^maxT
  495  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
  496 qlDiscountingPerpetualFuturesEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 =
  497   withYieldTermStructure a1 $ \a1' -> 
  498   withYieldTermStructure a2 $ \a2' -> 
  499   withQuote a3 $ \a3' -> 
  500   withDoubleArray a4 $ \(a4'1, a4'2) -> 
  501   withDoubleArray a5 $ \(a5'1, a5'2) -> 
  502   withDoubleArray a6 $ \(a6'1, a6'2) -> 
  503   let {a7' = fromEnumC a7} in 
  504   let {a8' = realToFrac a8} in 
  505   preErrorCheck $ \a9' -> 
  506   qlDiscountingPerpetualFuturesEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4'1  a4'2 a5'1  a5'2 a6'1  a6'2 a7' a8' a9' >>= \res ->
  507   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
  508   errorCheck  a9'>>
  509   return (res')
  510 
  511

  513 -- |discounts a bond's cash flows off a default-risky curve and a flat recovery rate
  514 riskyBondEngine :: (DefaultProbabilityTermStructure) -> (Double) -- ^recoveryRate
  515  -> (GenYieldTermStructure y) -> IO ((PricingEngine))
  516 riskyBondEngine a1 a2 a3 =
  517   withGenTermStructure a1 $ \a1' -> 
  518   let {a2' = realToFrac a2} in 
  519   withYieldTermStructure a3 $ \a3' -> 
  520   preErrorCheck $ \a4' -> 
  521   riskyBondEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' >>= \res ->
  522   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
  523   errorCheck  a4'>>
  524   return (res')

  528 -- |discounts a swap's legs off a single discount curve
  529 discountingSwapEngine :: (GenYieldTermStructure y) -> (Maybe Bool) -- ^includeSettlementDateFlows
  530  -> (Maybe Day) -- ^settlementDate
  531  -> (Maybe Day) -- ^npvDate
  532  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
  533 discountingSwapEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 =
  534   withYieldTermStructure a1 $ \a1' -> 
  535   let {a2' = fromMaybeBool a2} in 
  536   withMaybeDay a3 $ \a3' -> 
  537   withMaybeDay a4 $ \a4' -> 
  538   preErrorCheck $ \a5' -> 
  539   discountingSwapEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' >>= \res ->
  540   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
  541   errorCheck  a5'>>
  542   return (res')
  543

  546 -- |discounts an FX forward's two legs off their respective currency discount curves
  547 discountingFxForwardEngine :: (GenYieldTermStructure y1) -- ^sourceCurrencyDiscountCurve
  548  -> (GenYieldTermStructure y2) -- ^targetCurrencyDiscountCurve
  549  -> (GenQuote q) -- ^spotFx
  550  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
  551 discountingFxForwardEngine a1 a2 a3 =
  552   withYieldTermStructure a1 $ \a1' -> 
  553   withYieldTermStructure a2 $ \a2' -> 
  554   withQuote a3 $ \a3' -> 
  555   preErrorCheck $ \a4' -> 
  556   discountingFxForwardEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' >>= \res ->
  557   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
  558   errorCheck  a4'>>
  559   return (res')

  563 -- |Discounts each leg of a 'QuantLib.Instrument.Swap.ConstNotionalCrossCurrencySwap' (or either
  564 -- of its two leaves) off its own currency's discount curve, converting to @domesticCcy@ via
  565 -- @spotFX@ (quoted as units of @domesticCcy@ per unit of @foreignCcy@, w.r.t. a settlement equal
  566 -- to the npv date unless @spotFXSettleDate@ says otherwise). Each leg's stored currency must equal
  567 -- @domesticCcy@ or @foreignCcy@; the two discount curves must share the same reference date.
  568 discountingConstNotionalCrossCurrencySwapEngine :: (Currency) -- ^domesticCcy
  569  -> (GenYieldTermStructure y1) -- ^domesticCcyDiscountCurve
  570  -> (Currency) -- ^foreignCcy
  571  -> (GenYieldTermStructure y2) -- ^foreignCcyDiscountCurve
  572  -> (GenQuote q) -- ^spotFX
  573  -> (Maybe Bool) -- ^includeSettlementDateFlows
  574  -> (Maybe Day) -- ^settlementDate
  575  -> (Maybe Day) -- ^npvDate
  576  -> (Maybe Day) -- ^spotFXSettleDate
  577  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
  578 discountingConstNotionalCrossCurrencySwapEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 a9 =
  579   withCurrency a1 $ \a1' -> 
  580   withYieldTermStructure a2 $ \a2' -> 
  581   withCurrency a3 $ \a3' -> 
  582   withYieldTermStructure a4 $ \a4' -> 
  583   withQuote a5 $ \a5' -> 
  584   let {a6' = fromMaybeBool a6} in 
  585   withMaybeDay a7 $ \a7' -> 
  586   withMaybeDay a8 $ \a8' -> 
  587   withMaybeDay a9 $ \a9' -> 
  588   preErrorCheck $ \a10' -> 
  589   discountingConstNotionalCrossCurrencySwapEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' a9' a10' >>= \res ->
  590   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
  591   errorCheck  a10'>>
  592   return (res')

  596 -- | CVA/DVA-adjusted swap pricing engine. @invstDTS@\/@invstRecoveryRate@ are the
  597 -- own (investor-side) default probability curve and recovery rate for bilateral
  598 -- CVA\/DVA; pass 'Nothing' for @invstDTS@ and @0.999@ for @invstRecoveryRate@ to
  599 -- match upstream's unilateral-CVA-only defaults.
  600 counterpartyAdjSwapEngine :: (GenYieldTermStructure y) -- ^discountCurve
  601  -> (GenQuote q) -- ^blackVol
  602  -> (DefaultProbabilityTermStructure) -- ^ctptyDTS
  603  -> (Double) -- ^ctptyRecoveryRate
  604  -> (Maybe DefaultProbabilityTermStructure) -- ^invstDTS
  605  -> (Double) -- ^invstRecoveryRate
  606  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
  607 counterpartyAdjSwapEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 =
  608   withYieldTermStructure a1 $ \a1' -> 
  609   withQuote a2 $ \a2' -> 
  610   withGenTermStructure a3 $ \a3' -> 
  611   let {a4' = realToFrac a4} in 
  612   withMaybeDefaultProbabilityTermStructure a5 $ \a5' -> 
  613   let {a6' = realToFrac a6} in 
  614   preErrorCheck $ \a7' -> 
  615   counterpartyAdjSwapEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' >>= \res ->
  616   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
  617   errorCheck  a7'>>
  618   return (res')

  622 -- |analytic pricing engine for barrier options
  623 analyticBarrierEngine :: (GeneralizedBlackScholesProcess) -> IO ((PricingEngine))
  624 analyticBarrierEngine a1 =
  625   withGeneralizedBlackScholesProcess a1 $ \a1' -> 
  626   preErrorCheck $ \a2' -> 
  627   analyticBarrierEngine'_ a1' a2' >>= \res ->
  628   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
  629   errorCheck  a2'>>
  630   return (res')

  634 -- |analytic (Heynen and Kat) pricing engine for a barrier option on two assets, where the first asset's value is compared to the strike and the second's is monitored against the barrier
  635 analyticTwoAssetBarrierEngine :: (GeneralizedBlackScholesProcess) -- ^process1
  636  -> (GeneralizedBlackScholesProcess) -- ^process2
  637  -> (GenQuote q) -- ^rho
  638  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
  639 analyticTwoAssetBarrierEngine a1 a2 a3 =
  640   withGeneralizedBlackScholesProcess a1 $ \a1' -> 
  641   withGeneralizedBlackScholesProcess a2 $ \a2' -> 
  642   withQuote a3 $ \a3' -> 
  643   preErrorCheck $ \a4' -> 
  644   analyticTwoAssetBarrierEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' >>= \res ->
  645   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
  646   errorCheck  a4'>>
  647   return (res')

  651 -- |analytic pricing engine for soft barrier options, knocked in/out proportionally over a barrier range
  652 analyticSoftBarrierEngine :: (GeneralizedBlackScholesProcess) -> IO ((PricingEngine))
  653 analyticSoftBarrierEngine a1 =
  654   withGeneralizedBlackScholesProcess a1 $ \a1' -> 
  655   preErrorCheck $ \a2' -> 
  656   analyticSoftBarrierEngine'_ a1' a2' >>= \res ->
  657   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
  658   errorCheck  a2'>>
  659   return (res')

  663 -- |analytic pricing engine for simple chooser options
  664 analyticSimpleChooserEngine :: (GeneralizedBlackScholesProcess) -> IO ((PricingEngine))
  665 analyticSimpleChooserEngine a1 =
  666   withGeneralizedBlackScholesProcess a1 $ \a1' ->

  671   return (res')

  752 -- |/NB/ Timesteps for Cox-Ross-Rubinstein trees are adjusted using the Boyle-Lau algorithm;
  753 -- pass @maxTimeSteps = timeSteps@ to disable it, or @0@ to use the library's default heuristic.
  754 binomialBarrierEngine :: (BinomialTree) -> (GeneralizedBlackScholesProcess) -> (Word) -- ^timeSteps
  755  -> (Word) -- ^maxTimeSteps
  756  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
  757 binomialBarrierEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 =
  758   let {a1' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a1} in 
  759   withGeneralizedBlackScholesProcess a2 $ \a2' -> 
  760   let {a3' = fromIntegral a3} in 
  761   let {a4' = fromIntegral a4} in 
  762   preErrorCheck $ \a5' -> 
  763   binomialBarrierEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' >>= \res ->
  764   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
  765   errorCheck  a5'>>
  766   return (res')

  770 -- |FX barrier option engine using the vanna-volga method to account for the volatility smile
  771 vannaVolgaBarrierEngine :: (DeltaVolQuote) -- ^atmVol
  772  -> (DeltaVolQuote) -- ^vol25Put
  773  -> (DeltaVolQuote) -- ^vol25Call
  774  -> (GenQuote q) -- ^spotFX
  775  -> (GenYieldTermStructure y1) -- ^domesticTS
  776  -> (GenYieldTermStructure y2) -- ^foreignTS
  777  -> (Bool) -- ^adaptVanDelta
  778  -> (Double) -- ^bsPriceWithSmile
  779  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
  780 vannaVolgaBarrierEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 =
  781   withGenQuote a1 $ \a1' -> 
  782   withGenQuote a2 $ \a2' -> 
  783   withGenQuote a3 $ \a3' -> 
  784   withQuote a4 $ \a4' -> 
  785   withYieldTermStructure a5 $ \a5' -> 
  786   withYieldTermStructure a6 $ \a6' -> 
  787   let {a7' = C2HSImp.fromBool a7} in 
  788   let {a8' = realToFrac a8} in 
  789   preErrorCheck $ \a9' -> 
  790   vannaVolgaBarrierEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' a9' >>= \res ->
  791   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
  792   errorCheck  a9'>>
  793   return (res')
  794

  797 -- |analytic pricing engine for double-barrier European options
  798 analyticDoubleBarrierEngine :: (GeneralizedBlackScholesProcess) -> (Int) -- ^series
  799  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
  800 analyticDoubleBarrierEngine a1 a2 =
  801   withGeneralizedBlackScholesProcess a1 $ \a1' -> 
  802   let {a2' = fromIntegral a2} in 
  803   preErrorCheck $ \a3' -> 
  804   analyticDoubleBarrierEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' >>= \res ->
  805   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
  806   errorCheck  a3'>>
  807   return (res')

  811 -- |always uses 'AnalyticDoubleBarrierEngine' as the underlying smile-free double-barrier engine
  812 vannaVolgaDoubleBarrierEngine :: (DeltaVolQuote) -- ^atmVol
  813  -> (DeltaVolQuote) -- ^vol25Put
  814  -> (DeltaVolQuote) -- ^vol25Call
  815  -> (GenQuote q) -- ^spotFX
  816  -> (GenYieldTermStructure y1) -- ^domesticTS
  817  -> (GenYieldTermStructure y2) -- ^foreignTS
  818  -> (Bool) -- ^adaptVanDelta
  819  -> (Double) -- ^bsPriceWithSmile
  820  -> (Int) -- ^series
  821  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
  822 vannaVolgaDoubleBarrierEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 a9 =
  823   withGenQuote a1 $ \a1' -> 
  824   withGenQuote a2 $ \a2' -> 
  825   withGenQuote a3 $ \a3' -> 
  826   withQuote a4 $ \a4' -> 
  827   withYieldTermStructure a5 $ \a5' -> 
  828   withYieldTermStructure a6 $ \a6' -> 
  829   let {a7' = C2HSImp.fromBool a7} in 
  830   let {a8' = realToFrac a8} in 
  831   let {a9' = fromIntegral a9} in 
  832   preErrorCheck $ \a10' -> 
  833   vannaVolgaDoubleBarrierEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' a9' a10' >>= \res ->
  834   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
  835   errorCheck  a10'>>
  836   return (res')

  855 -- |Monte Carlo pricing engine for double-barrier options
  856 mcDoubleBarrierEngine :: (RngTrait) -> (StatisticsTrait) -> (GeneralizedBlackScholesProcess) -> (Maybe Word) -- ^timeSteps
  857  -> (Maybe Word) -- ^timeStepsPerYear
  858  -> (Bool) -- ^brownianBridge
  859  -> (Bool) -- ^antitheticVariate
  860  -> (Maybe Word) -- ^requiredSamples
  861  -> (Maybe Double) -- ^requiredTolerance
  862  -> (Maybe Word) -- ^maxSamples
  863  -> (Word) -- ^seed
  864  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
  865 mcDoubleBarrierEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 a9 a10 a11 =
  866   let {a1' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a1} in 
  867   let {a2' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a2} in 
  868   withGeneralizedBlackScholesProcess a3 $ \a3' -> 
  869   let {a4' = fromMaybeInt a4} in 
  870   let {a5' = fromMaybeInt a5} in 
  871   let {a6' = C2HSImp.fromBool a6} in 
  872   let {a7' = C2HSImp.fromBool a7} in 
  873   let {a8' = fromMaybeInt a8} in 
  874   let {a9' = fromMaybeDouble a9} in 
  875   let {a10' = fromMaybeInt a10} in 
  876   let {a11' = fromIntegral a11} in 
  877   preErrorCheck $ \a12' -> 
  878   mcDoubleBarrierEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' a9' a10' a11' a12' >>= \res ->
  879   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
  880   errorCheck  a12'>>
  881   return (res')

 1092 -- |Monte Carlo pricing engine for forward-starting European options under a Black-Scholes process
 1093 mcForwardEuropeanBSEngine :: (RngTrait) -> (StatisticsTrait) -> (GeneralizedBlackScholesProcess) -> (Maybe Word) -- ^timeSteps
 1094  -> (Maybe Word) -- ^timeStepsPerYear
 1095  -> (Bool) -- ^brownianBridge
 1096  -> (Bool) -- ^antitheticVariate
 1097  -> (Maybe Word) -- ^requiredSamples
 1098  -> (Maybe Double) -- ^requiredTolerance
 1099  -> (Maybe Word) -- ^maxSamples
 1100  -> (Word) -- ^seed
 1101  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
 1102 mcForwardEuropeanBSEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 a9 a10 a11 =
 1103   let {a1' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a1} in 
 1104   let {a2' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a2} in 
 1105   withGeneralizedBlackScholesProcess a3 $ \a3' -> 
 1106   let {a4' = fromMaybeInt a4} in 
 1107   let {a5' = fromMaybeInt a5} in 
 1108   let {a6' = C2HSImp.fromBool a6} in 
 1109   let {a7' = C2HSImp.fromBool a7} in 
 1110   let {a8' = fromMaybeInt a8} in 
 1111   let {a9' = fromMaybeDouble a9} in 
 1112   let {a10' = fromMaybeInt a10} in 
 1113   let {a11' = fromIntegral a11} in 
 1114   preErrorCheck $ \a12' -> 
 1115   mcForwardEuropeanBSEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' a9' a10' a11' a12' >>= \res ->
 1116   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
 1117   errorCheck  a12'>>
 1118   return (res')

 1166 -- |quanto-adjusts a European vanilla option's price and greeks for a payoff paid in a currency other than the underlying's; binds the @VanillaOption@\/@AnalyticEuropeanEngine@ instantiation of upstream's @QuantoEngine\<Instr,Engine\>@ template
 1167 quantoEuropeanEngine :: (GeneralizedBlackScholesProcess) -> (GenYieldTermStructure y) -- ^foreignRiskFreeRate
 1168  -> (GenBlackVolTermStructure bv) -- ^exchangeRateVolatility
 1169  -> (GenQuote q) -- ^correlation
 1170  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
 1171 quantoEuropeanEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 =
 1172   withGeneralizedBlackScholesProcess a1 $ \a1' -> 
 1173   withYieldTermStructure a2 $ \a2' -> 
 1174   withBlackVolTermStructure a3 $ \a3' -> 
 1175   withQuote a4 $ \a4' -> 
 1176   preErrorCheck $ \a5' -> 
 1177   quantoEuropeanEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' >>= \res ->
 1178   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
 1179   errorCheck  a5'>>
 1180   return (res')

 1285 -- |shifted-lognormal Black-formula swaption engine, taking a flat volatility quote
 1286 blackSwaptionEngine :: (GenYieldTermStructure y) -> (GenQuote q) -> (DayCounter) -> (Double) -- ^displacement
 1287  -> (CashAnnuityModel) -- ^model
 1288  -> IO ((PricingEngine))

 1292   withDayCounter a3 $ \a3' ->

 1296   blackSwaptionEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' >>= \res ->
 1297   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
 1298   errorCheck  a6'>>
 1299   return (res')

 1463 -- |cap\/floor pricing engine for any one-factor Gaussian short-rate model, evaluated by
 1464 -- integration over the model's state variable. As 'gaussian1dSwaptionEngine', without
 1465 -- 'Probabilities'.
 1466 gaussian1dCapFloorEngine :: (Gaussian1dModel) -> (Int) -- ^integrationPoints
 1467  -> (Double) -- ^stddevs
 1468  -> (Bool) -- ^extrapolatePayoff
 1469  -> (Bool) -- ^flatPayoffExtrapolation
 1470  -> (Maybe (GenYieldTermStructure y)) -- ^discountCurve
 1471  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
 1472 gaussian1dCapFloorEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 =
 1473   withStandalone a1 $ \a1' -> 
 1474   let {a2' = fromIntegral a2} in 
 1475   let {a3' = realToFrac a3} in 
 1476   let {a4' = C2HSImp.fromBool a4} in 
 1477   let {a5' = C2HSImp.fromBool a5} in 
 1478   withMaybeYieldTermStructure a6 $ \a6' -> 
 1479   preErrorCheck $ \a7' -> 
 1480   gaussian1dCapFloorEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' >>= \res ->
 1481   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
 1482   errorCheck  a7'>>
 1483   return (res')

 1680 -- |semi-analytic Bates-model pricing engine, integrating with a fixed relative tolerance and evaluation cap
 1681 batesEngine' :: (GenBatesModel bm) -> (Double) -- ^relTolerance
 1682  -> (Word) -- ^maxEvaluations
 1683  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
 1684 batesEngine' a1 a2 a3 =
 1685   withBatesModel a1 $ \a1' -> 
 1686   let {a2' = realToFrac a2} in 
 1687   let {a3' = fromIntegral a3} in 
 1688   preErrorCheck $ \a4' -> 
 1689   batesEngine''_ a1' a2' a3' a4' >>= \res ->
 1690   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
 1691   errorCheck  a4'>>
 1692   return (res')

 1810 -- |CDS pricing engine that integrates the default-leg payoff over the CDS's step-wise schedule
 1811 integralCdsEngine :: ((Word,TimeUnit)) -- ^integrationStep
 1812  -> (DefaultProbabilityTermStructure) -> (Double) -- ^recoveryRate
 1813  -> (GenYieldTermStructure y) -- ^discountCurve
 1814  -> (Maybe Bool) -- ^includeSettlementDateFlows
 1815  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
 1816 integralCdsEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 =
 1817   let {(a1'1, a1'2) = fromEnumQuantity a1} in 
 1818   withGenTermStructure a2 $ \a2' -> 
 1819   let {a3' = realToFrac a3} in 
 1820   withYieldTermStructure a4 $ \a4' -> 
 1821   let {a5' = fromMaybeBool a5} in 
 1822   preErrorCheck $ \a6' -> 
 1823   integralCdsEngine'_ a1'1  a1'2 a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' >>= \res ->
 1824   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
 1825   errorCheck  a6'>>
 1826   return (res')

 1855 -- |swaption pricing engine for any one-factor Gaussian short-rate model, evaluated by integration over the model's state variable
 1856 gaussian1dSwaptionEngine :: (Gaussian1dModel) -> (Int) -- ^integrationPoints
 1857  -> (Double) -- ^stddevs
 1858  -> (Bool) -- ^extrapolatePayoff
 1859  -> (Bool) -- ^flatPayoffExtrapolation
 1860  -> (Maybe (GenYieldTermStructure y)) -- ^discountCurve
 1861  -> (Probabilities) -- ^probabilities
 1862  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
 1863 gaussian1dSwaptionEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 =
 1864   withStandalone a1 $ \a1' -> 
 1865   let {a2' = fromIntegral a2} in 
 1866   let {a3' = realToFrac a3} in 
 1867   let {a4' = C2HSImp.fromBool a4} in 
 1868   let {a5' = C2HSImp.fromBool a5} in 
 1869   withMaybeYieldTermStructure a6 $ \a6' -> 
 1870   let {a7' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a7} in 
 1871   preErrorCheck $ \a8' -> 
 1872   gaussian1dSwaptionEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' >>= \res ->
 1873   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
 1874   errorCheck  a8'>>
 1875   return (res')

 1994 -- |CDS pricing engine implementing the ISDA standard model
 1995 isdaCdsEngine :: (DefaultProbabilityTermStructure) -> (Double) -- ^recoveryRate
 1996  -> (GenYieldTermStructure y) -> (Maybe Bool) -- ^includeSettlementDateFlows
 1997  -> (NumericalFix) -- ^numericalFix
 1998  -> (AccrualBias) -- ^accrualBias
 1999  -> (ForwardsInCouponPeriod) -- ^forwardsInCouponPeriod
 2000  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
 2001 isdaCdsEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 =
 2002   withGenTermStructure a1 $ \a1' -> 
 2003   let {a2' = realToFrac a2} in 
 2004   withYieldTermStructure a3 $ \a3' -> 
 2005   let {a4' = fromMaybeBool a4} in 
 2006   let {a5' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a5} in 
 2007   let {a6' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a6} in 
 2008   let {a7' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a7} in 
 2009   preErrorCheck $ \a8' -> 
 2010   isdaCdsEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' >>= \res ->
 2011   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
 2012   errorCheck  a8'>>
 2013   return (res')

 2017 -- |variance-swap pricing engine using a replicating portfolio of vanilla options at the given strikes
 2018 replicatingVarianceSwapEngine :: (GeneralizedBlackScholesProcess) -> (Double) -- ^dk
 2019  -> (NonEmpty Double) -- ^callStrikes
 2020  -> (NonEmpty Double) -- ^putStrikes
 2021  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
 2022 replicatingVarianceSwapEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 =
 2023   withGeneralizedBlackScholesProcess a1 $ \a1' -> 
 2024   let {a2' = realToFrac a2} in 
 2025   withNonEmptyDoubleArray a3 $ \(a3'1, a3'2) -> 
 2026   withNonEmptyDoubleArray a4 $ \(a4'1, a4'2) -> 
 2027   preErrorCheck $ \a5' -> 
 2028   replicatingVarianceSwapEngine'_ a1' a2' a3'1  a3'2 a4'1  a4'2 a5' >>= \res ->
 2029   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
 2030   errorCheck  a5'>>
 2031   return (res')

 2110 -- |Snapshots @rTS@/@fTS@/@fxVolTS@ at construction time (their underlying @shared_ptr@s are copied
 2111 -- out of their handles): a later relink of a 'RelinkableYieldTermStructure' or
 2112 -- 'RelinkableBlackVolTermStructure' passed in here will /not/ be reflected in this 'FdmQuantoHelper'.
 2113 fdmQuantoHelper :: (GenYieldTermStructure y1) -- ^rTS
 2114  -> (GenYieldTermStructure y2) -- ^fTS
 2115  -> (GenBlackVolTermStructure bv) -- ^fxVolTS
 2116  -> (Double) -- ^equityFxCorrelation
 2117  -> (Double) -- ^exchRateATMlevel
 2118  -> IO ((FdmQuantoHelper))
 2119 fdmQuantoHelper a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 =
 2120   withYieldTermStructure a1 $ \a1' -> 
 2121   withYieldTermStructure a2 $ \a2' -> 
 2122   withBlackVolTermStructure a3 $ \a3' -> 
 2123   let {a4' = realToFrac a4} in 
 2124   let {a5' = realToFrac a5} in 
 2125   preErrorCheck $ \a6' -> 
 2126   fdmQuantoHelper'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' >>= \res ->
 2127   peekFdmQuantoHelper res >>= \res' ->
 2128   errorCheck  a6'>>
 2129   return (res')

 2134 fdmQuantoHelperQuantoAdjustment :: (FdmQuantoHelper) -> (Double) -- ^equityVol
 2135  -> (Double) -- ^t1
 2136  -> (Double) -- ^t2
 2137  -> IO ((Double))

 2139   withFdmQuantoHelper a1 $ \a1' -> 
 2140   let {a2' = realToFrac a2} in 
 2141   let {a3' = realToFrac a3} in 
 2142   let {a4' = realToFrac a4} in 
 2143   preErrorCheck $ \a5' -> 
 2144   fdmQuantoHelperQuantoAdjustment'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' >>= \res ->
 2145   let {res' = realToFrac res} in
 2146   errorCheck  a5'>>
 2147   return (res')

 2154 -- |finite-differences swaption pricing engine for the G2 two-factor short-rate model

 2156  -> (Word) -- ^xGrid
 2157  -> (Word) -- ^yGrid
 2158  -> (Word) -- ^dampingSpecs
 2159  -> (Double) -- ^invEps

 2164   let {a3' = fromIntegral a3} in 
 2165   let {a4' = fromIntegral a4} in 
 2166   let {a5' = fromIntegral a5} in 
 2167   let {a6' = realToFrac a6} in 
 2168   withFdmSchemeDesc a7 $ \a7' -> 
 2169   preErrorCheck $ \a8' -> 
 2170   fdG2SwaptionEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' >>= \res ->
 2171   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
 2172   errorCheck  a8'>>

 2297 -- |/NB/ every Monte Carlo engine in this module is C++-templated on both an RNG policy and a
 2298 -- statistics accumulator; all of them (bar 'mcAmericanBasketEngine', see its own doc comment)
 2299 -- take an explicit 'StatisticsTrait' argument for the latter, letting the caller pick
 2300 -- 'Statistics'\/'GaussianStatistics'\/'GeneralStatistics'\/'IncrementalStatistics' instead of being
 2301 -- pinned to upstream's default @Statistics@.
 2302 mcHestonHullWhiteEngine :: (RngTrait) -> (StatisticsTrait) -> (HybridHestonHullWhiteProcess) -> (Maybe Word) -- ^timeSteps
 2303  -> (Maybe Word) -- ^timStepsPerYear
 2304  -> (Bool) -- ^antitheticVariate
 2305  -> (Bool) -- ^controlVariate
 2306  -> (Maybe Word) -- ^requiredSamples
 2307  -> (Maybe Double) -- ^requiredTolerance
 2308  -> (Maybe Word) -- ^maxSamples
 2309  -> (Word) -- ^seed
 2310  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
 2311 mcHestonHullWhiteEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 a9 a10 a11 =
 2312   let {a1' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a1} in 
 2313   let {a2' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a2} in 
 2314   withGenStochasticProcess a3 $ \a3' -> 
 2315   let {a4' = fromMaybeInt a4} in 
 2316   let {a5' = fromMaybeInt a5} in 
 2317   let {a6' = C2HSImp.fromBool a6} in 
 2318   let {a7' = C2HSImp.fromBool a7} in 
 2319   let {a8' = fromMaybeInt a8} in 
 2320   let {a9' = fromMaybeDouble a9} in 
 2321   let {a10' = fromMaybeInt a10} in 
 2322   let {a11' = fromIntegral a11} in 
 2323   preErrorCheck $ \a12' -> 
 2324   mcHestonHullWhiteEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' a9' a10' a11' a12' >>= \res ->
 2325   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
 2326   errorCheck  a12'>>

 2639  -> IO ((PricingEngine))

 2643   withGenStochasticProcess a3 $ \a3' -> 
 2644   let {a4' = C2HSImp.fromBool a4} in 
 2645   let {a5' = C2HSImp.fromBool a5} in

 2647   let {a7' = fromMaybeDouble a7} in 
 2648   let {a8' = fromMaybeInt a8} in 
 2649   let {a9' = fromIntegral a9} in 
 2650   preErrorCheck $ \a10' -> 
 2651   mcHimalayaEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' a9' a10' >>= \res ->

 2714 -- |Monte Carlo (least-squares) pricing engine for an American 'basketOption'. /NB/ unlike every
 2715 -- other MC engine in this module, this one has no 'StatisticsTrait' parameter: upstream's
 2716 -- @MCAmericanBasketEngine\<RNG\>@ is templated on @RNG@ only -- its base
 2717 -- @MCLongstaffSchwartzEngine\<BasketOption::engine,MultiVariate,RNG\>@ never forwards a second
 2718 -- template argument, so there is no @S@ to expose here (a real upstream limitation, not an
 2719 -- oversight).
 2720 mcAmericanBasketEngine :: (RngTrait) -> (StochasticProcessArray) -> (Maybe Word) -- ^timeSteps
 2721  -> (Maybe Word) -- ^timeStepsPerYear
 2722  -> (Bool) -- ^brownianBridge
 2723  -> (Bool) -- ^antitheticVariate
 2724  -> (Maybe Word) -- ^requiredSamples
 2725  -> (Maybe Double) -- ^requiredTolerance
 2726  -> (Maybe Word) -- ^maxSamples
 2727  -> (Word) -- ^seed
 2728  -> (Maybe Word) -- ^nCalibrationSamples
 2729  -> (Word) -- ^polynomialOrder
 2730  -> (PolynomialType) -- ^polynomialType
 2731  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
 2732 mcAmericanBasketEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 a9 a10 a11 a12 a13 =
 2733   let {a1' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a1} in 
 2734   withGenStochasticProcess a2 $ \a2' -> 
 2735   let {a3' = fromMaybeInt a3} in 
 2736   let {a4' = fromMaybeInt a4} in 
 2737   let {a5' = C2HSImp.fromBool a5} in 
 2738   let {a6' = C2HSImp.fromBool a6} in 
 2739   let {a7' = fromMaybeInt a7} in 
 2740   let {a8' = fromMaybeDouble a8} in 
 2741   let {a9' = fromMaybeInt a9} in 
 2742   let {a10' = fromIntegral a10} in 
 2743   let {a11' = fromMaybeInt a11} in 
 2744   let {a12' = fromIntegral a12} in 
 2745   let {a13' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a13} in 
 2746   preErrorCheck $ \a14' -> 
 2747   mcAmericanBasketEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' a9' a10' a11' a12' a13' a14' >>= \res ->
 2748   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
 2749   errorCheck  a14'>>
 2750   return (res')

 3097  -> (Word) -- ^dampingSteps
 3098  -> (FdmScheme) -> IO ((PricingEngine))
 3099 fdBlackScholesShoutEngine' a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 =
 3100   withGeneralizedBlackScholesProcess a1 $ \a1' -> 
 3101   withDividendArray a2 $ \(a2'1, a2'2) -> 
 3102   let {a3' = fromIntegral a3} in 
 3103   let {a4' = fromIntegral a4} in 
 3104   let {a5' = fromIntegral a5} in 
 3105   withFdmSchemeDesc a6 $ \a6' -> 
 3106   preErrorCheck $ \a7' -> 
 3107   fdBlackScholesShoutEngine''_ a1' a2'1  a2'2 a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' >>= \res ->
 3108   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
 3109   errorCheck  a7'>>
 3110   return (res')

 3167 -- |finite-differences pricing engine for vanilla options combining a Heston equity model with a Hull-White short-rate model
 3168 fdHestonHullWhiteVanillaEngine :: (GenHestonModel hm) -> (HullWhiteProcess) -> (Double) -- ^corrEquityShortRate
 3169  -> (Word) -- ^tGrid
 3170  -> (Word) -- ^xGrid
 3171  -> (Word) -- ^vGrid
 3172  -> (Word) -- ^rGrid
 3173  -> (Word) -- ^dampingSteps
 3174  -> (Bool) -- ^controlVariate, upstream default: true

 3176 fdHestonHullWhiteVanillaEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 a9 a10 =

 3181   let {a5' = fromIntegral a5} in 
 3182   let {a6' = fromIntegral a6} in 
 3183   let {a7' = fromIntegral a7} in 
 3184   let {a8' = fromIntegral a8} in 
 3185   let {a9' = C2HSImp.fromBool a9} in 
 3186   withFdmSchemeDesc a10 $ \a10' -> 
 3187   preErrorCheck $ \a11' -> 
 3188   fdHestonHullWhiteVanillaEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' a9' a10' a11' >>= \res ->
 3189   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
 3190   errorCheck  a11'>>
 3191   return (res')

 3657 -- |Black-Scholes-Merton option-price calculator, from the option type and strike directly
 3658 blackScholesCalculator' :: (OptionType) -> (Double) -- ^strike
 3659  -> (Double) -- ^spot
 3660  -> (Double) -- ^growth
 3661  -> (Double) -- ^stdDev
 3662  -> (Double) -- ^discount
 3663  -> IO ((BlackScholesCalculator))
 3664 blackScholesCalculator' a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 =
 3665   let {a1' = fromEnumC a1} in 
 3666   let {a2' = realToFrac a2} in 
 3667   let {a3' = realToFrac a3} in 
 3668   let {a4' = realToFrac a4} in 
 3669   let {a5' = realToFrac a5} in 
 3670   let {a6' = realToFrac a6} in 
 3671   preErrorCheck $ \a7' -> 
 3672   blackScholesCalculator''_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' >>= \res ->
 3673   peekBlackScholesCalculator res >>= \res' ->
 3674   errorCheck  a7'>>
 3675   return (res')

 3776   let {a5' = realToFrac a5} in

 3778   bachelierCalculator''_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' >>= \res ->
 3779   peekBachelierCalculator res >>= \res' ->
 3780   errorCheck  a6'>>
 3781   return (res')

 4070  -> (Double) -- ^fDiscount (foreign discount factor)
 4071  -> (Double) -- ^stdDev
 4072  -> IO ((BlackDeltaCalculator))
 4073 blackDeltaCalculator a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 =
 4074   let {a1' = fromEnumC a1} in

 4081   blackDeltaCalculator'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' >>= \res ->
 4082   peekBlackDeltaCalculator res >>= \res' ->
 4083   errorCheck  a7'>>
 4084   return (res')

 4190 -- |Black 1976 probability of being in the money (in the bond martingale measure), i.e. N(d2). It is a risk-neutral probability, not the real world one. /Warning/ instead of volatility it uses standard deviation, i.e. volatility*sqrt(timeToMaturity)
 4191 blackCashItmProbability :: (OptionType) -> (Double) -> (Double) -- ^forward
 4192  -> (Double) -- ^stdDev
 4193  -> (Double) -- ^displacement
 4194  -> IO ((Double))
 4195 blackCashItmProbability a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 =
 4196   let {a1' = fromEnumC a1} in 
 4197   let {a2' = realToFrac a2} in 
 4198   let {a3' = realToFrac a3} in 
 4199   let {a4' = realToFrac a4} in 
 4200   let {a5' = realToFrac a5} in 
 4201   preErrorCheck $ \a6' -> 
 4202   blackCashItmProbability'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' >>= \res ->
 4203   let {res' = realToFrac res} in
 4204   errorCheck  a6'>>
 4205   return (res')

 4624 -- |initial guess (alpha, beta, nu, rho) for SABR calibration, per Le Floc'h and Kennedy
 4625 sabrGuess :: (Double) -- ^k_m
 4626  -> (Double) -- ^vol_m
 4627  -> (Double) -- ^k_0
 4628  -> (Double) -- ^vol_0
 4629  -> (Double) -- ^k_p
 4630  -> (Double) -- ^vol_p
 4631  -> (Double) -- ^forward
 4632  -> (Double) -- ^expiryTime
 4633  -> (Double) -- ^beta
 4634  -> (Double) -- ^shift
 4635  -> (VolatilityType) -- ^volatilityType
 4636  -> IO (([Double]))
 4637 sabrGuess a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 a9 a10 a11 =
 4638   let {a1' = realToFrac a1} in 
 4639   let {a2' = realToFrac a2} in 
 4640   let {a3' = realToFrac a3} in 
 4641   let {a4' = realToFrac a4} in 
 4642   let {a5' = realToFrac a5} in 
 4643   let {a6' = realToFrac a6} in 
 4644   let {a7' = realToFrac a7} in 
 4645   let {a8' = realToFrac a8} in 
 4646   let {a9' = realToFrac a9} in 
 4647   let {a10' = realToFrac a10} in 
 4648   let {a11' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a11} in 
 4649   preArray $ \(a12'1, a12'2) -> 
 4650   preErrorCheck $ \a13' -> 
 4651   sabrGuess'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' a9' a10' a11' a12'1  a12'2 a13' >>
 4652   peekDoubleArray  a12'1  a12'2>>= \a12'' -> 
 4653   errorCheck  a13'>>
 4654   return (a12'')
